+// Each thread owns four rows of a 32x32 tile. This keeps the tile-wide
+// synchronization cost low while avoiding a 1024-thread block.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                          unsigned int* const path,
+                                          const size_t numNodes,
+                                          const size_t round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            tile[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            tile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                tile[row][intermediate] + tile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < tile[row][column]) {
+                tile[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = tile[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+// Update one tile in the pivot row or pivot column. The pivot tile is already
+// closed over all intermediate vertices in the current round.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                                   unsigned int* const path,
+                                                   const size_t numNodes,
+                                                   const size_t round,
+                                                   const size_t targetTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotTile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const bool rowPhase = blockIdx.x < (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t actualTargetTile = rowPhase
+        ? static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)
+        : static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) - tileCount;
+
+    // The pivot tile was handled by the first phase. Returning the whole block
+    // here is safe because this branch is uniform for every thread.
+    if (actualTargetTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance = rowPhase
+                ? pivotTile[row][intermediate] + target[intermediate][column]
+                : target[row][intermediate] + pivotTile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Update all tiles that are neither in the pivot row nor in the pivot column.
+// The row and column tiles produced by phase two are read-only inputs here.
+__global__ void floydWarshallRemainderKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                              unsigned int* const path,
+                                              const size_t numNodes,
+                                              const size_t round) {
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t rowTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y);
+    const size_t columnTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (rowTile == round || columnTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = dist[globalRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + globalColumn];
+        } else {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                rowTileData[row][intermediate] + columnTileData[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, unsigned int* const devicePath,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);
+
+    for (size_t round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
+        floydWarshallPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        const unsigned int rowColumnBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount * 2);
+        floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel<<<rowColumnBlocks, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round, 0);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        const dim3 remainderGrid(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+        floydWarshallRemainderKernel<<<remainderGrid, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}

     // Basic sanity checks

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                               const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (expression); \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaStatus, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)

+// Each thread owns four rows of a 32x32 tile. This keeps the tile-wide
+// synchronization cost low while avoiding a 1024-thread block.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                          unsigned int* const path,
+                                          const size_t numNodes,
+                                          const size_t round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            tile[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            tile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                tile[row][intermediate] + tile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < tile[row][column]) {
+                tile[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = tile[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+// Update one tile in the pivot row or pivot column. The pivot tile is already
+// closed over all intermediate vertices in the current round.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                                   unsigned int* const path,
+                                                   const size_t numNodes,
+                                                   const size_t round,
+                                                   const size_t targetTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotTile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const bool rowPhase = blockIdx.x < (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t actualTargetTile = rowPhase
+        ? static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)
+        : static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) - tileCount;
+
+    // The pivot tile was handled by the first phase. Returning the whole block
+    // here is safe because this branch is uniform for every thread.
+    if (actualTargetTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance = rowPhase
+                ? pivotTile[row][intermediate] + target[intermediate][column]
+                : target[row][intermediate] + pivotTile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Update all tiles that are neither in the pivot row nor in the pivot column.
+// The row and column tiles produced by phase two are read-only inputs here.
+__global__ void floydWarshallRemainderKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                              unsigned int* const path,
+                                              const size_t numNodes,
+                                              const size_t round) {
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t rowTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y);
+    const size_t columnTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (rowTile == round || columnTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = dist[globalRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + globalColumn];
+        } else {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                rowTileData[row][intermediate] + columnTileData[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, unsigned int* const devicePath,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);
+
+    for (size_t round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
+        floydWarshallPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        const unsigned int rowColumnBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount * 2);
+        floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel<<<rowColumnBlocks, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round, 0);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        const dim3 remainderGrid(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+        floydWarshallRemainderKernel<<<remainderGrid, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}
+

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                               const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (expression); \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaStatus, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \

+// Each thread owns four rows of a 32x32 tile. This keeps the tile-wide
+// synchronization cost low while avoiding a 1024-thread block.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                          unsigned int* const path,
+                                          const size_t numNodes,
+                                          const size_t round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            tile[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            tile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                tile[row][intermediate] + tile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < tile[row][column]) {
+                tile[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = tile[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+// Update one tile in the pivot row or pivot column. The pivot tile is already
+// closed over all intermediate vertices in the current round.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                                   unsigned int* const path,
+                                                   const size_t numNodes,
+                                                   const size_t round,
+                                                   const size_t targetTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotTile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const bool rowPhase = blockIdx.x < (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t actualTargetTile = rowPhase
+        ? static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)
+        : static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) - tileCount;
+
+    // The pivot tile was handled by the first phase. Returning the whole block
+    // here is safe because this branch is uniform for every thread.
+    if (actualTargetTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance = rowPhase
+                ? pivotTile[row][intermediate] + target[intermediate][column]
+                : target[row][intermediate] + pivotTile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Update all tiles that are neither in the pivot row nor in the pivot column.
+// The row and column tiles produced by phase two are read-only inputs here.
+__global__ void floydWarshallRemainderKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                              unsigned int* const path,
+                                              const size_t numNodes,
+                                              const size_t round) {
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t rowTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y);
+    const size_t columnTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (rowTile == round || columnTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = dist[globalRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + globalColumn];
+        } else {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                rowTileData[row][intermediate] + columnTileData[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, unsigned int* const devicePath,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);
+
+    for (size_t round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
+        floydWarshallPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        const unsigned int rowColumnBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount * 2);
+        floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel<<<rowColumnBlocks, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round, 0);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        const dim3 remainderGrid(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+        floydWarshallRemainderKernel<<<remainderGrid, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}

    24	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
    25	                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    26	    unsigned int seed = 42;
    27	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    28	    
    29	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
    30	        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    31	    }
    32	    
    33	    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    34	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    35	        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    36	    }
    37	}
    38	
    39	void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    40	    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
    41	        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    42	            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
    43	            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
    44	        }
    45	        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    46	    }
    47	}

   177	            const unsigned int newDistance = rowPhase
   178	                ? pivotTile[row][intermediate] + target[intermediate][column]
   179	                : target[row][intermediate] + pivotTile[intermediate][column];
   180	            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
   181	                target[row][column] = newDistance;
   182	                localPath[rowOffset] =
   183	                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
   184	            }
   185	        }
   186	        __syncthreads();
   187	    }
   188	
   189	    #pragma unroll
   190	    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
   191	        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
   192	        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
   193	                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
   194	        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
   195	                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
   196	        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
   197	            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
   198	            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
   199	            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
   200	        }
   201	    }
   202	}
   203	
   204	// Update all tiles that are neither in the pivot row nor in the pivot column.
   205	// The row and column tiles produced by phase two are read-only inputs here.
   206	__global__ void floydWarshallRemainderKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
   207	                                              unsigned int* const path,
   208	                                              const size_t numNodes,
   209	                                              const size_t round) {
   210	    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
   211	    const size_t rowTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y);
   212	    const size_t columnTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
   213	    if (rowTile == round || columnTile == round) {
   214	        return;
   215	    }
   216	
   217	    __shared__ unsigned int rowTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
   218	    __shared__ unsigned int columnTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
   219	    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
   220	
   221	    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
   222	    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
   223	    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
   224	
   225	    #pragma unroll
   226	    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
   227	        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
   228	        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
   229	        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
   230	        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
   231	        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
   232	
   233	        if (globalRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
   234	            rowTileData[row][column] = dist[globalRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
   235	        } else {
   236	            rowTileData[row][column] = INF;
   237	        }
   238	
   239	        if (pivotRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
   240	            columnTileData[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + globalColumn];
   241	        } else {
   242	            columnTileData[row][column] = INF;
   243	        }
   244	
   245	        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
   246	            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
   247	            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
   248	            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
   249	        } else {
   250	            target[row][column] = INF;
   251	        }
   252	    }
   253	    __syncthreads();
   254	
   255	    #pragma unroll
   256	    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
   257	        #pragma unroll
   258	        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
   259	            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
   260	            const unsigned int newDistance =
   261	                rowTileData[row][intermediate] + columnTileData[intermediate][column];
   262	            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
   263	                target[row][column] = newDistance;
   264	                localPath[rowOffset] =
   265	                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
   266	            }
   267	        }
   268	        __syncthreads();
   269	    }
   270	
   271	    #pragma unroll
   272	    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
   273	        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
   274	        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
   275	        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
   276	        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
   277	            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
   278	            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
   279	            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
   280	        }
   281	    }
   282	}
   283	
   284	void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, unsigned int* const devicePath,
   285	                   const size_t numNodes) {
   286	    if (numNodes == 0) {
   287	        return;
   288	    }
   289	
   290	    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
   291	    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);
   292	
   293	    for (size_t round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
   294	        floydWarshallPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
   295	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   296	
   297	        const unsigned int rowColumnBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount * 2);
   298	        floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel<<<rowColumnBlocks, block>>>(
   299	            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round, 0);
   300	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   301	
   302	        const dim3 remainderGrid(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
   303	                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
   304	        floydWarshallRemainderKernel<<<remainderGrid, block>>>(
   305	            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
   306	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   307	    }
   308	
   309	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   310	}
   311	
   312	bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
   313	    // Basic sanity checks
   314	    
   315	    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
   316	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   317	        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
   318	            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
   319	            return false;
   320	        }
   321	    }
   322	    
   323	    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
   324	    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
   325	    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
   326	        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
   327	            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
   328	                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
   329	                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
   330	                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
   331	                
   332	                // Check for overflow before addition
   333	                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
   334	                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
   335	                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
   336	                               i, j, k);
   337	                        return false;
   338	                    }
   339	                }
   340	            }
   341	        }
   342	    }
   343	    
   344	    return true;
   345	}
   346	
   347	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   348	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   349	    printf("Options:\n");
   350	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
   351	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   352	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   353	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   354	}
   355	
   356	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   357	    size_t numNodes = 512;
   358	    bool validate = false;
   359	    bool printResults = false;
   360	    
   361	    // Parse command line arguments
   362	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   363	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   364	            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
   365	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   366	            validate = true;
   367	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   368	            printResults = true;
   369	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   370	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   371	            return 0;
   372	        } else {
   373	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   374	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   375	            return 1;
   376	        }
   377	    }
   378	    
   379	    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
   380	    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
   381	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   382	    
   383	    // Allocate matrices
   384	    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
   385	    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
   386	    
   387	    // Initialize
   388	    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
   389	    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
   390	    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
   391	    
   392	    const size_t matrixElements = numNodes * numNodes;
   393	    const size_t matrixBytes = matrixElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
   394	    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
   395	    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
   396	
   397	    if (matrixElements != 0) {
   398	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceDist), matrixBytes));
   399	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&devicePath), matrixBytes));
   400	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, dist.data(), matrixBytes,
   401	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   402	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, path.data(), matrixBytes,
   403	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    // Run the CUDA Floyd-Warshall implementation. Host/device transfers are
   407	    // outside the timed region so the reported time measures GPU computation.
   408	    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
   409	    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
   410	    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
   411	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
   412	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
   413	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
   414	
   415	    floydWarshall(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes);
   416	
   417	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
   418	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
   419	    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   420	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
   421	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
   422	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
   423	
   424	    if (matrixElements != 0) {
   425	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dist.data(), deviceDist, matrixBytes,
   426	                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   427	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(path.data(), devicePath, matrixBytes,
   428	                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   429	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
   430	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                               const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (expression); \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaStatus, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)

+// Each thread owns four rows of a 32x32 tile. This keeps the tile-wide
+// synchronization cost low while avoiding a 1024-thread block.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                          unsigned int* const path,
+                                          const size_t numNodes,
+                                          const size_t round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            tile[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            tile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                tile[row][intermediate] + tile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < tile[row][column]) {
+                tile[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = tile[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+// Update one tile in the pivot row or pivot column. The pivot tile is already
+// closed over all intermediate vertices in the current round.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                                   unsigned int* const path,
+                                                   const size_t numNodes,
+                                                   const size_t round,
+                                                   const size_t targetTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotTile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const bool rowPhase = blockIdx.x < (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t actualTargetTile = rowPhase
+        ? static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)
+        : static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) - tileCount;
+
+    // The pivot tile was handled by the first phase. Returning the whole block
+    // here is safe because this branch is uniform for every thread.
+    if (actualTargetTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance = rowPhase
+                ? pivotTile[row][intermediate] + target[intermediate][column]
+                : target[row][intermediate] + pivotTile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Update all tiles that are neither in the pivot row nor in the pivot column.
+// The row and column tiles produced by phase two are read-only inputs here.
+__global__ void floydWarshallRemainderKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                              unsigned int* const path,
+                                              const size_t numNodes,
+                                              const size_t round) {
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t rowTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y);
+    const size_t columnTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (rowTile == round || columnTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = dist[globalRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + globalColumn];
+        } else {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                rowTileData[row][intermediate] + columnTileData[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}

+void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, unsigned int* const devicePath,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);
+
+    for (size_t round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
+        floydWarshallPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        const unsigned int rowColumnBlocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount * 2);
+        floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel<<<rowColumnBlocks, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round, 0);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+
+        const dim3 remainderGrid(static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount),
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+        floydWarshallRemainderKernel<<<remainderGrid, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                               const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (expression); \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaStatus, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)

+// Each thread owns four rows of a 32x32 tile. This keeps the tile-wide
+// synchronization cost low while avoiding a 1024-thread block.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                          unsigned int* const path,
+                                          const size_t numNodes,
+                                          const size_t round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            tile[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            tile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                tile[row][intermediate] + tile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < tile[row][column]) {
+                tile[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
             }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = tile[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
     }
 }
 
+// Update one tile in the pivot row or pivot column. The pivot tile is already
+// closed over all intermediate vertices in the current round.
+__global__ void floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                                   unsigned int* const path,
+                                                   const size_t numNodes,
+                                                   const size_t round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivotTile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const bool rowPhase = blockIdx.x < (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const size_t actualTargetTile = rowPhase
+        ? static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)
+        : static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) - tileCount;
+
+    // The pivot tile was handled by the first phase. Returning the whole block
+    // here is safe because this branch is uniform for every thread.
+    if (actualTargetTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            pivotTile[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance = rowPhase
+                ? pivotTile[row][intermediate] + target[intermediate][column]
+                : target[row][intermediate] + pivotTile[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t targetRow = (rowPhase ? round : actualTargetTile) * TILE_SIZE
+                               + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t targetColumn = (rowPhase ? actualTargetTile : round) * TILE_SIZE
+                                  + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (targetRow < numNodes && targetColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = targetRow * numNodes + targetColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Update all tiles that are neither in the pivot row nor in the pivot column.
+// The row and column tiles produced by phase two are read-only inputs here.
+__global__ void floydWarshallRemainderKernel(unsigned int* const dist,
+                                              unsigned int* const path,
+                                              const size_t numNodes,
+                                              const size_t round) {
+    const size_t rowTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y);
+    const size_t columnTile = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (rowTile == round || columnTile == round) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ unsigned int rowTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int columnTileData[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int target[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int firstRow = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    unsigned int localPath[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        const size_t pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && pivotColumn < numNodes) {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = dist[globalRow * numNodes + pivotColumn];
+        } else {
+            rowTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (pivotRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = dist[pivotRow * numNodes + globalColumn];
+        } else {
+            columnTileData[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            target[row][column] = dist[matrixIndex];
+            localPath[rowOffset] = path[matrixIndex];
+        } else {
+            target[row][column] = INF;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int intermediate = 0; intermediate < TILE_SIZE; ++intermediate) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+            const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+            const unsigned int newDistance =
+                rowTileData[row][intermediate] + columnTileData[intermediate][column];
+            if (newDistance < target[row][column]) {
+                target[row][column] = newDistance;
+                localPath[rowOffset] =
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(round * TILE_SIZE + intermediate);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++rowOffset) {
+        const int row = firstRow + rowOffset * THREADS_Y;
+        const size_t globalRow = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(row);
+        const size_t globalColumn = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + static_cast<size_t>(column);
+        if (globalRow < numNodes && globalColumn < numNodes) {
+            const size_t matrixIndex = globalRow * numNodes + globalColumn;
+            dist[matrixIndex] = target[row][column];
+            path[matrixIndex] = localPath[rowOffset];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, unsigned int* const devicePath,
+                   const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 block(THREADS_X, THREADS_Y);
+
+    for (size_t round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
+        floydWarshallPivotKernel<<<1, block>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+

+        floydWarshallPivotRowColumnKernel<<<rowColumnBlocks, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(tileCount));
+        floydWarshallRemainderKernel<<<remainderGrid, block>>>(
+            deviceDist, devicePath, numNodes, round);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}

     // Basic sanity checks

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                               const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (expression); \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) { \
+            cudaFailure(cudaStatus, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
